# Habio – Daily Habit Tracker (App Store ID 1517360968) — Full Review Analysis

**Gototop LTD · `com.habio` · "Routine planner & To do list"**

Every one of the **2,051 reviews** in `reviews.jsonl` was read individually, in its original language, and hand-coded. Nothing was sampled, keyword-filtered or skipped. Corpus: **2020-10-18 → 2026-07-30**, **81 storefronts**, **19 languages**, corpus mean **2.35★**.

---

## How to read this

- Every quantified claim carries **count, percentage, denominator, scope, period, signal band and review IDs**. Signal bands are those in `GENERALIZED_STORE_REVIEW_ANALYSIS_PROMPT.md`.
- Theme counts are **non-exclusive**: a review carrying six ideas is counted in six themes. Percentages are *shares of reviews mentioning a thing* and sum past 100%.
- **§10 is a complete per-review index.** Any number here resolves to exact reviews; any review resolves to the findings it supports via §9.J.
- Review IDs appear as `` `11376403157` `` (the `review_id` in `reviews.jsonl`) and as `#1309` (position in date order, 1…2,051). Both appear in §10.
- Quotations are verbatim and short. Non-English text is quoted in the original with a translation. `…` marks an elision.
- Global percentages use **all 2,051 reviews**, as the analysis spec requires. Where the suspected-seeded reviews (§0.7) change the picture, the share among the **1,462 non-seeded reviews** is shown beside it and labelled.

### ⚠️ Nine warnings before any number here is used

1. **This corpus is not the app's rating.** The corpus mean is **2.35★**. The US public rating returned by Apple's lookup API on 2026-09-14 is **4.28★ from 3,932 ratings** (§9.E). The two populations are different; neither is "the truth".
2. **The corpus is bimodal, not average.** 1,295 one-star reviews (63.14%) and 610 five-star (29.74%); only 146 reviews (7.12%) sit in between. A mean of 2.35★ describes almost nobody.
3. **589 reviews (28.72%) match a solicited or seeded pattern** (§0.7, §9.H). They are counted in every global number, as the spec requires, and they supply 81.59% of all praise codes. Read any positive global share next to its non-seeded share.
4. **The flag is a judgement, not proof.** It rests on content, timing, storefront, author-script and duplication tests (§9.H) that agree with each other. It cannot identify who wrote a review, and a few genuine short 5★ reviews may be caught in it.
5. **Most reviews describe billing, not the app.** 1033 reviews (50.37%) describe a charge, cancellation or refund problem; among non-seeded reviews that is 70.66%. Product-feature evidence is thin by comparison and is labelled so.
6. **Claims about charges are the reviewer's account.** No bank statement, receipt or support log is in the data. Recurrence across 81 storefronts and 19 languages is what gives the pattern weight, not any single review.
7. **The corpus is concentrated in one year.** Era E4 (2024-03-01 → 2025-02-19) holds 1,198 reviews (58.41%); E5 holds only 22. Late-period percentages are directional only.
8. **A review is not a user, and storefront is not nationality.** 5 author names appear twice (§9.H); 178 English-language reviews sit on de/fr/es/pl/ru/br/ae/sa storefronts.
9. **No conversion, revenue or refund rate is derivable.** 995 reviewers say money left their account (§5), a self-selected 48.51% of reviewers.

---

# PART 0 — READ THIS FIRST

The eight findings that should change a decision.

## 0.1 This review corpus is, above all, a record of unwanted charges

`U_BILLING_HARM` **878** reviews, **42.81%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.04★, 2021-10-08 → 2026-07-11. Among the 1,462 non-seeded reviews the share is **60.05%**. The reviewer calls the app a scam or fraud in `ACC_SCAM` **682** reviews, **33.25%** of 2,051, mean 1.00★.

The union is made of distinct, separately coded mechanisms, not one vague grievance:

| Code | Meaning | n | % of 2,051 | Band | Mean ★ |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| `MON_CHARGED_AFTER` | Charged after cancelling / deleting | 351 | 17.11% | High-priority signal | 1.04 |
| `MON_TRIAL_CHARGE` | Trial converted to full charge unexpectedly | 173 | 8.43% | High-priority signal | 1.03 |
| `MON_WORKBOOK` | Workbook / PDF / report upsell charge | 189 | 9.22% | High-priority signal | 1.01 |
| `MON_CHARGED_NOSUB` | Charged with no subscription / without authorisation | 164 | 8.00% | High-priority signal | 1.03 |
| `MON_DOUBLECHARGE` | Multiple / duplicate charges | 157 | 7.65% | High-priority signal | 1.08 |
| `MON_PRICE_MISMATCH` | Charged more than the price shown | 55 | 2.68% | Meaningful signal | 1.04 |
| `MON_REFUND_DENIED` | Refund refused | 242 | 11.80% | High-priority signal | 1.01 |
| `MON_CANCEL` | Cannot cancel / cancellation hard | 361 | 17.60% | High-priority signal | 1.01 |
| `MON_OFFSTORE` | Subscription outside App Store (not in Apple subscriptions) | 137 | 6.68% | High-priority signal | 1.01 |
| `BANK_DISPUTE` | Bank / card / PayPal dispute or card cancelled | 118 | 5.75% | High-priority signal | 1.08 |
| `REG_COMPLAINT` | Regulator / police / legal / Apple removal threat | 94 | 4.58% | Very strong signal | 1.00 |

> "They make money based on a $1 3 day trial period, and if you forget to cancel in that window, they charge you $40 and NO MATTER WHAT they aren’t giving you that money back" — #1309, `11376403157`, us, 1★

> "Shame on the developers for scamming people, like me, who are trying to work on their mental health." — #400, `10362168353`, us, 1★

The two most-voted reviews in the corpus (#400, `10362168353`, us, 1★, 16 votes; #521, `10607823887`, us, 1★, 13 votes) are both this complaint. `BANK_DISPUTE` **118** reviews, **5.75%** of 2,051 report a bank, card or PayPal dispute or a cancelled card.

**Evidence:** `7891448469`, `10214635678`, `10420955834`, `10694976664`, `10840288375`, `10981564707`, `11118431115`, `11201312848`, `11349818317`, `11579635106`.

## 0.2 The cancellation path lives outside the App Store, and customers cannot find it

`U_CANCEL_TRAP` **597** reviews, **29.11%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.03★, 2021-12-18 → 2026-07-11. Inside it, `MON_OFFSTORE` **137** reviews, **6.68%** of 2,051 say the subscription **does not appear in iOS Subscriptions**, so Apple cannot cancel or refund it, and `MON_WEB_FUNNEL` **30** reviews, **1.46%** of 2,051 say they paid on the website quiz before installing.

> "all payments in the Habio application are not displayed in the AppStore and the receipt comes directly from Habio, i.e. the application charges the payment bypassing the AppStore" — #318, `10212536465`, ru, 1★

> "the subscription is not linked to your Apple account, so even after you delete the app you will still be charged $50 every 180 days unless you redownload the app and cancel through there" — #2043, `13184471122`, us, 1★

> "The app sends email a link which you need to click within 20 mins else it expires." — #543, `10647307854`, au, 1★

The store listing tells customers the opposite (external, §9.E): *"Payment will be charged to iTunes Account at confirmation of purchase"* and *"Subscriptions may be managed by the user and auto-renewal may be turned off by going to the user's Account Settings after purchase."* That text may be accurate for in-app purchases. For the web-funnel purchases the reviewers describe, it is not. People who delete the app believing that cancels it make up `CHURN_DELETE` **66** reviews, **3.22%** of 2,051.

**Evidence:** `9744192670`, `10186376860`, `10291927094`, `10458944163`, `10670144338`, `10812907599`, `10943620287`, `11116988648`, `11333056698`, `11503641580`.

## 0.3 A $29.99 "workbook" is charged from a single tap during sign-up

`MON_WORKBOOK` **189** reviews, **9.22%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.01★, 2023-10-15 → 2025-05-04. It first appears on 2023-10-15 (#459, `10476768777`, cz, 1★) and peaks in 2024-04 with 41 reviews. Reviewers describe a "download" or "Get my PDF" button shown straight after the trial payment, charged against the card that was just authorised, with no second confirmation:

> "The consent to charge text is in such a low contrast colour with the background, and such a tiny type size that it hurts my eyes to even read it!" — #926, `11079433591`, gb, 1★

> "la vérification est demandé donc ils ne la redemande pas ensuite" — #1288, `11359863927`, fr, 1★
> *(translation: the verification is requested at subscription, so they don't request it again afterwards)*

Duplicate charges appear in `MON_DOUBLECHARGE` **157** reviews, **7.65%** of 2,051, often two workbooks ("adhd_workbook" + "habits_workbook") from pressing a button that seemed not to respond. Two reviewers paste support's reply word for word (#1095, `11188294921`, mx, 1★; #1191, `11245047696`, es, 1★):

> "Please note that we do not offer refunds for in-app purchases." — #1095, `11188294921`, mx, 1★

> "At that point I hadn’t even downloaded the app HOW on earth could I have made in app purchases ?!" — #695, `10844663395`, us, 1★

**Evidence:** `10476768777`, `10759152490`, `10844663395`, `10937556792`, `11018225433`, `11125248895`, `11165313527`, `11241965115`, `11383294185`, `11603758221`.

## 0.4 The ads and the quiz promise things the app does not have

`U_AD_MISMATCH` **195** reviews, **9.51%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.08★, 2021-12-30 → 2025-07-10. It breaks into three distinct promises:

| Code | Meaning | n | % of 2,051 | Band | Mean ★ |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| `MKT_LANG_BAIT` | Ad/quiz in local language, app English-only | 86 | 4.19% | Very strong signal | 1.07 |
| `MKT_AD_PERSONALITY` | Ad/quiz promised personalised report/plan/profile | 31 | 1.51% | Meaningful signal | 1.26 |
| `MKT_AD_BIONIC` | Ad promised bionic / bold-letter reading tool | 22 | 1.07% | Meaningful signal | 1.00 |
| `MKT_AD_MISLEAD` | Ad did not match the product | 99 | 4.83% | Very strong signal | 1.02 |

- **Language.** Every `MKT_LANG_BAIT` review is dated **2024-03-28 or later**: the ad and quiz ran in French, Spanish, German, Arabic or Japanese, then the paid app turned out English-only. Apple's lookup API lists one language, EN, yet the listing titles are localised ("Habio: Tagesplaner. Checkliste", "Habio: Habitude et Objectif", "Healthy Habits & Goal tracker").
- **A reading tool.** Instagram ads showed "bionic" bold-first-letter reading for ADHD, from 2023-07-18 to 2024-09-01. No such feature exists: "What a bait and switch!!" (#298, `10182376089`, us, 1★).
- **A personalised report.** The quiz promised a Myers-Briggs or ADHD "profile", report or tailored plan: "Nothing like that exists in the app." (#127, `8602506980`, us, 1★).

> "L’ensemble des publicités ansi que les questionnaires avant l’achat sont en français, et l’application n’est dispo qu’en anglais." — #1167, `11225362605`, fr, 1★
> *(translation: All the ads and the pre-purchase questionnaires are in French, and the app is only available in English.)*

**Evidence:** `8185587507`, `10237694854`, `10682701692`, `10866726722`, `11115922049`, `11144431975`, `11201564180`, `11253611923`, `11426352050`, `11623582096`.

## 0.5 The audience the ads target is the audience that reports the most harm

`USE_ADHD` **97** reviews, **4.73%** of 2,051 — Very strong signal, mean 1.25★, 2022-05-10 → 2026-05-11, below even the non-seeded corpus mean (1.31★). Inside the ADHD segment: `MON_REFUND_DENIED` 34 of 97 ADHD reviewers (35.05%), `MKT_AD_MISLEAD` 30 of 97 ADHD reviewers (30.93%), `NEG_DARKUX` 23 of 97 ADHD reviewers (23.71%). Their charge that the design exploits inattention is specific, not rhetorical:

> "They use lack of focus among the targeted ADHD group and trick You into in app purchase without confirming payment!" — #1562, `11655316652`, pl, 1★

> "en sachant pertinemment à quel point le TDAH est difficile à vivre au quotidien notamment vis à vis de la gestion financière" — #491, `10557400319`, fr, 1★
> *(translation: knowing full well how hard ADHD is to live with day to day, especially when it comes to managing money)*

Vulnerability is named outright (disability, low income, student, mental-health crisis) in `USE_ACCESS` **18** reviews, **0.88%** of 2,051; mental health is the context in `USE_MENTAL` **40** reviews, **1.95%** of 2,051.

**Evidence:** `8657686337`, `10200455380`, `10479057312`, `10759152490`, `10904111593`, `11017250240`, `11136312427`, `11221025226`, `11400851068`, `11637134228`.

## 0.6 Support is slower than the trial it is meant to cancel

`U_SUPPORT_FAIL` **425** reviews, **20.72%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.04★, 2021-01-12 → 2026-07-11. `SUP_NONE` **213** reviews, **10.39%** of 2,051; `SUP_SLOW` **78** reviews, **3.80%** of 2,051; `SUP_TEMPLATE` **59** reviews, **2.88%** of 2,051; `SUP_EVASIVE` **46** reviews, **2.24%** of 2,051. Reviewers name a published response time of up to four business days (19 reviews quote it) against a trial of three days:

> "How convenient when they offer a 3-day trial" — #1262, `11333056698`, us, 1★

> "On 7/28 @ 3:23p I received a response asking if the" — #317, `10212438359`, us, 1★

> "Funny how when it’s public your response is timely." — #260, `10093276946`, us, 1★

The same agent name recurs in 5 reviews (#428 #499 #838 #1095 #1278). Reviewers mention a templated public developer reply under their own or others' reviews in 19 reviews (§9.I). Developer responses themselves are not in the dataset.

**Evidence:** `6862196911`, `10158875434`, `10391338268`, `10670064212`, `10809192566`, `10904499795`, `11028445019`, `11191647532`, `11336340431`, `11585253726`.

## 0.7 589 five- and four-star reviews look solicited, and they coincide with a public rating far above the corpus

`SUS_SEED` **589** reviews, **28.72%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 4.91★, 2024-03-11 → 2025-02-18. Hand-flagged while reading. The flag was then tested against signals that were **not used to assign it** (§9.H):

| Test | Flagged group | Non-flagged 4–5★ | 1–3★ |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Reviews | 589 | 99 | 1,364 |
| Author name in Cyrillic script | **198 (33.6%)** | 1 (1.0%) | 8 (0.6%) |
| On the US storefront | 555 (94.2%) | 25 (25.3%) | 307 (22.5%) |
| Any helpfulness vote | 18 (3.1%) | 28 (28.3%) | 213 (15.6%) |
| Date range | 2024-03-11 → 2025-02-18 | 2020-10-18 → 2026-04-11 | 2020-11-10 → 2026-07-30 |
| Mentions a charge, refund, cancellation or support | 0 | 4 | 1,029 |

Beyond the statistics there are artefacts that genuine reviews do not produce:

- **A developer reply pasted into a 5★ body**: "Habio keeps me motivated and on top of We are thrilled to hear that Arch." (#1846, `12076539872`, us, 5★). "Arch" is the title of #1607, `11686908710`, us, 5★.
- **One paragraph split across three accounts in the same hour**: #1799, `12012446802`, us, 5★ (title "Habio stands out because of its ability to let users track as many habits as they choose."), then #1800, `12012499702`, us, 5★ ("This flexibility, combined with daily motivational quotes and a reflective journal"…), then #1801, `12012575477`, us, 5★.
- **Six bodies posted twice by different accounts, four days apart** (`SUS_DUPTEXT` **12** reviews, **0.59%** of 2,051), matching a mechanical duplicate-text test exactly.
- **Referral spam**: "GuruApp code 9x8xj0" (#1289, `11360249558`, us, 5★).
- **Praise aimed precisely at the complaint**: `SUS_COUNTER` **31** reviews, **1.51%** of 2,051: "Cancelled my subscription with no issues at all" (#1285, `11357158616`, us, 5★); "Customer support is quick and helpful." (#1459, `11554629461`, us, 5★).
- **Praise for things that are not happening**: `PR_UPDATES` (5 reviews, 2024-09-23 → 2024-11-13) praises frequent updates, while the current version is dated **2024-05-15**. `PR_SOCIAL` (4) praises sharing with friends, which the listing advertises; no non-seeded reviewer mentions it.

Genuine reviewers noticed it at the time: "The app has 4.2 stars, but I have not read more than 2 positive reviews" (#312, `10210418380`, au, 1★); "These scammers pay to have their ratings boosted - hence their 4 star rating." (#992, `11130425750`, ca, 1★).

**What this means for every other number:** the non-seeded corpus has mean **1.31★** against 4.91★ for the flagged group. Of the 668 reviews carrying any praise code, 545 are flagged. The flag does **not** remove a single complaint.

**Evidence:** `11032875249`, `11215847195`, `11465033271`, `11558490250`, `11646235418`, `11729390299`, `11791593827`, `11887928662`, `12012499702`, `12083990766`, `12126667236`, `12194423051`.

## 0.8 What real customers valued is small, but it is there

Among the 1,462 non-seeded reviews, praise of any kind appears in **123 (8.41%)**. 39 of those are from the free era E1. What survives is consistent: the calm visual design (`PR_DESIGN` 39 non-seeded), and the idea (`PR_CONCEPT` 14), often praised *by the people complaining*:

> "I love the look and feel of the app, it’s calming" — #2045, `13626218768`, za, 1★

> "beautiful soft graphics and layout which make it easy to look at and not overwhelming" — #183, `9076061818`, gb, 5★

> "I liked it so much I purchased a yearly subscription" — #2032, `12435693615`, us, 3★

The same #2032, `12435693615`, us, 3★ review ends "This IS gonna cause me to cancel !" because of support. The 2026 dashboard review #2045, `13626218768`, za, 1★ shows the design being undermined by upsell: "a mini Time Square Billboard showcase". The product has a small real base of appreciation. The business model around it is what the corpus condemns.

**Evidence:** `6547367313`, `6863614321`, `7168337466`, `7915137610`, `8676998514`, `8984118160`, `9984700446`, `10499399735`, `10865199427`, `11304376291`.

# PART 1 — SCOPE AND METHOD

## 1.1 Files used

| File | Role | Records |
|---|---|---:|
| `app store review/67. Habio - Daily Habit Tracker - Routine planner & To do list/reviews.jsonl` | **the corpus**; every claim resolves here | 2,051 |
| `by_country/*.jsonl` | 81 per-storefront files | 2,051 (union) |
| `manifest.json` | extraction metadata, per-country counts, rating distribution | — |
| `_state.json` | collection state per polled storefront | — |
| Apple iTunes Lookup API (external, 2026-09-14) | public rating, listing text, prices, languages, current version | 8 storefronts |

## 1.2 Schema actually present

All 13 fields are present on all 2,051 records; no title or body is empty.

| Field | Used for | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `review_id` | primary key | 2,051 distinct; zero duplicates |
| `country`, `country_name` | §6 | 81 storefronts |
| `rating` | §4 | integers 1–5 only |
| `title`, `body` | **read in full for every record** | |
| `author` | authenticity tests only (§9.H) | zero repeat (country, author) pairs; 5 names on two storefronts |
| `date` | §7 | 2020-10-18 → 2026-07-30 |
| `vote_count`, `vote_sum` | §9.H | 259 reviews have votes; max 16 |
| `is_edited` | §9.H | 38 edited |
| `app_id`, `app_name` | constant (`1517360968`, `Habio - Daily Habit Tracker`) | |

**Not present, and therefore never claimed:** app version per review, purchase records, developer responses, device, and any identifier linking a reviewer across storefronts.

## 1.3 Coverage and reconciliation

`67-reconcile.py` output, verbatim:

```
main records                 : 2051
distinct review_id           : 2051
duplicate review_id          : 0 []
exact content duplicates     : 0
repeat (storefront,author)   : 0
authors on >1 storefront     : 5
by_country files             : 81
sum of country file records  : 2051
country files == main subset : YES
storefronts in main          : 81
manifest total_reviews       : 2051  match=True
manifest storefronts         : 81  match=True
manifest per-country mismatch: 0 []
manifest rating dist match   : True  ({1: 1295, 2: 44, 3: 25, 4: 77, 5: 610})
corpus mean rating           : 2.3481  manifest 2.348  match=True
_state storefronts probed    : 153
_state all complete          : True
_state collected mismatch    : 0 []
_state zero-review fronts    : 72
sum _state collected         : 2051
field presence               : {'review_id': 2051, 'app_id': 2051, 'app_name': 2051, 'country': 2051, 'country_name': 2051, 'rating': 2051, 'title': 2051, 'body': 2051, 'author': 2051, 'date': 2051, 'vote_count': 2051, 'vote_sum': 2051, 'is_edited': 2051}
empty title / body           : 0 / 0
ratings outside 1-5          : 0
date range                   : 2020-10-18T08:15:14Z -> 2026-07-30T06:01:54Z
app_id values                : {'1517360968'}
app_name values              : {'Habio - Daily Habit Tracker'}
is_edited true               : 38
vote_count>0                 : 259, max 16
vote_sum negative            : 0
```

**Reviews read: 2,051/2,051 = 100%. Reviews hand-coded: 2,051/2,051 = 100%.** No deduplication was applied: there are no technical duplicates. The 12 duplicated *bodies* are different review IDs from different accounts and are kept and flagged (§9.H).

## 1.4 Processing method

1. `67-dump.py` emits all 2,051 reviews in date order with a stable index (1…2,051).
2. Reviews were read in **21 batches of up to 100**, in order, in full, in the original language (en, es, fr, de, pt, ko, ar, ru, it, vi, pl, tr, zh, uk, da, ja, fi, sv, nl). Where a translation is quoted, it was checked against the source string.
3. Each review received hand codes plus a note quoting the text that drove them (`67-cls/*.txt`, 2,051 lines).
4. **After every batch**, `67-check-cls.py` re-asserted each line's `review_id` against the corpus at that index. It reported zero drift. One mistyped ID was caught while writing (§9.C).
5. `67-build-classification.py` rejects unknown codes, adds mechanical codes (`STAR_n`, `ERA_En`) from the record, and derives 14 unions.
6. `67-validate.py` runs **765 checks**; 0 fail (§9.C).
7. `67-build-report.py` computes every number and chooses every representative ID from the data at build time. It **refuses to write** if any quotation is not found verbatim in the review it is attributed to. No number or review ID in this report was typed by hand.
8. `67-verify-report.py` re-reads the written file and checks it against the corpus (§9.M).

**Classification is hand judgement, not an algorithm.** No clustering, keyword rule or model assigned a code. Every line in `67-cls/` carries a note quoting its evidence, so any code can be audited.

## 1.5 Signal thresholds

| Share of reviews | Label |
|---|---|
| < 0.1% | Ignore by default |
| 0.1% – < 0.5% | Weak signal |
| 0.5% – < 1% | Emerging signal |
| 1% – < 3% | Meaningful signal |
| 3% – 5% | Very strong signal |
| > 5% | High-priority signal |

At n=2,051, 0.1% ≈ 2 reviews and 5% ≈ 103 reviews. Segment rates (within a star band, era, country or the ADHD/paid segments) are labelled as such and show both denominators.

## 1.6 Limitations, stated plainly

- **Selection.** People write reviews after being charged far more readily than after a quiet good week. The corpus measures grievance intensity, not prevalence among users.
- **Unverifiable claims.** Charges, amounts and support exchanges are as reported. Consistency across hundreds of independent reviewers in many languages is the evidence; no single review is proof.
- **Authenticity flag.** `SUS_SEED` is a judgement corroborated by independent tests (§9.H), not a determination. Global numbers include the flagged reviews; non-seeded figures are shown where they matter.
- **No version data.** Release history per review is absent; the only version fact is the current version and its date from the lookup API.
- **Thin late period.** E5 has 22 reviews; nothing after 2025-02 supports a trend claim.
- **External pages.** apps.apple.com returned an empty page to the fetch; listing facts come from Apple's lookup API only.

# PART 2 — PRODUCT AND MONETIZATION MODEL

## 2.1 What the product is, from the reviews

A habit tracker with preset habits and routines, a daily check-off, a reflection journal, short articles and courses ("insights"), motivational quotes, reminders and simple progress stats. Customers arrive mostly through a **web quiz reached from Instagram or Facebook ads**, pay a small trial fee on the web or in the app, and are then offered further purchases (workbooks, reports) before or during first use.

| Capability | Evidence in non-seeded reviews | Note |
|---|---|---|
| Preset habits / routines, check-off | `NEG_NO_CUSTOM_HABIT` 9, `REQ_CUSTOM_ROUTINE` 12 | own routines cannot be created or edited, #1099, `11189301426`, us, 1★ |
| Reflection journal | `PR_JOURNAL` 5 non-seeded / 45 total | #2046, `13944588671`, us, 5★ |
| Articles, courses, quotes | `PR_CONTENT` 12 vs `NEG_CONTENT_THIN` 57 | criticised far more than praised |
| Reminders | `PR_NOTIF` 0 non-seeded, `BUG_NOTIF` 6 | |
| Onboarding quiz | `NEG_ONBOARD_LONG` 29, `MKT_AD_PERSONALITY` 31 | quiz doubles as the sales funnel |
| Workbooks / PDF reports | `MON_WORKBOOK` 189, `BUG_CONTENT` 10 | paid add-on, often not received |
| Localisation | `REQ_LANG` 41, `MKT_LANG_BAIT` 86 | English only (§9.E) |

## 2.2 What the store listing says (external, Apple Lookup API, accessed 2026-09-14)

- Seller **Gototop LTD**; genre Health & Fitness; age rating 4+; released 2020-10-07; current version **4.3.1**, dated **2024-05-15**.
- Languages declared: **EN**.
- Listing claims (verbatim): *"designed in collaboration with mental health professionals"*; *"Opportunity to share Insights and achievements with friends"*; *"The app is free to download. We also offer an optional subscription package."*
- The US description lists **13 distinct price points** from $0.49 to $79.99: weekly, monthly, quarterly and yearly subscriptions with and without 3-day trials, plus *"a one-time non-consumable purchase for $49.99, which unlocks all functionality."* The workbook price reviewers name most ($29.99) is not described as a workbook.

| Storefront | Listing title | Public mean ★ | Ratings |
|---|---|---:|---:|
| us | Habio - Daily Habit Tracker | 4.28 | 3,932 |
| gb | Habio: Routine & Habit Tracker | 3.68 | 896 |
| ca | Habio: Habit Tracker & Planner | 4.04 | 550 |
| au | Habio: Habit Tracker & Planner | 3.71 | 407 |
| fr | Habio: Habitude et Objectif | 3.05 | 302 |
| de | Habio: Tagesplaner. Checkliste | 2.65 | 291 |
| mx | Healthy Habits & Goal tracker | 3.31 | 176 |
| es | Habio - Daily Habit Tracker | 2.93 | 153 |

## 2.3 Free / paid / trial / unclear — classified

| Capability or offer | Classification | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| Early app (2020–2021) | **Free** with limited free tier | `PR_FREE` 9 (first 2020-12-10), `MON_FREE_LIMIT` 10: "This app doesn’t ask for payments which I really love" (#6, `6736531333`, ae, 5★) |
| Using the app (2022 →) | **Paid; hard paywall at the end of onboarding** | `MON_HARDWALL` 47, 33 in E2: "“Optional subscription” was a lie" (#77, `8254767500`, us, 1★) |
| Trial | **Paid trial**, typically $1/€1/£1 for 3 days or $5–$10 for 1–2 weeks, auto-converting | `MON_TRIAL_1DOLLAR` 148, `MON_TRIAL_CHARGE` 173 |
| Premium content inside the app after paying | **Partly further-gated** | `MON_UPSELL` 10: "even i payed for subscription 350 kr but everything is locked" (#496, `10568318776`, se, 1★) |
| Workbooks / reports / PDFs | **One-off paid add-on**, ~$29.99 each | `MON_WORKBOOK` 189 |
| Lifetime / one-time unlock | **Listed ($49.99); no reviewer says they bought it** | text sweep for lifetime/one-time wording finds 4 reviews (#18 #606 #1019 #1156), none a purchase; `MON_ONETIME_REQ` 2 ask for one |
| Web-funnel subscription | **Paid off-store** (card/PayPal), per reviewers | `MON_WEB_FUNNEL` 30, `MON_OFFSTORE` 137 |

## 2.4 What could not be established

- The split between App Store in-app purchases and web purchases, and whether the off-store reports come only from web purchases. The corpus contains both ("on peut très bien se désabonner via le Store" *(you can unsubscribe perfectly well via the Store)* (#1244, `11304376291`, fr, 4★)).
- Whether the workbook screen changed. `MON_WORKBOOK` collapses after 2024-09 while review volume also falls.
- The price actually charged in each case, beyond reviewers' statements (§9.G).

## 3.2 What goes wrong — billing, in detail

### Charged after cancelling, or without subscribing (high-priority)

`MON_CHARGED_AFTER` **351** reviews, **17.11%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.04★, 2022-02-24 → 2025-09-25. `MON_CHARGED_NOSUB` **164** reviews, **8.00%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.03★, 2023-07-28 → 2025-05-09. The recurring detail is a cancellation that the customer believed complete: an in-app confirmation, deleting the app, deleting the account, or an email to support.

> "While the app does show you a confirmation screen upon submitting your cancelation request, the app itself does not update to show a static message about your canceled status." — #521, `10607823887`, us, 1★

> "I receive a charge (6) months later in February 1st 2024 from habio." — #854, `11011058403`, ca, 5★

> "Tuve que investigar que aplicaciones tenía GOTOTOP LTD para saber que me estaban cobrando" — #1740, `11858280315`, mx, 1★
> *(translation: I had to research which apps GOTOTOP LTD owns to find out who was charging me)*

**Evidence:** `8392560675`, `10093276946`, `10307254617`, `10477352849`, `10758134621`, `10940829711`, `11176393209`, `11482491207`; `10193187378`, `10734970574`, `10996455665`, `11167095774`, `11247655488`, `11589220826`.

### Trial converts before the customer expects, or without a reminder (high-priority)

`MON_TRIAL_CHARGE` **173** reviews, **8.43%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.03★, 2021-10-08 → 2025-01-15. `MON_NO_RECEIPT` **68** reviews, **3.32%** of 2,051 say no reminder or receipt preceded the charge; `MON_TRIAL_SHORT` **9** reviews, **0.44%** of 2,051 say the window was too short or shorter than support's reply time; `MON_PRICE_MISMATCH` **55** reviews, **2.68%** of 2,051 say the amount exceeded the price shown.

> "The day before my trial ended, they took the money and THEN emailed me and said my trial was up the following day." — #1437, `11521787038`, us, 1★

> "Factually their 3 days trial for 1,00 Euro is actually a 2 two days trial" — #284, `10164682229`, nl, 1★

**Evidence:** `7891448469`, `10230841817`, `10571106959`, `10756364742`, `10986195996`, `11136886688`, `11228834364`, `11516818049`.

### Refunds refused, and the money-back guarantee (high-priority)

`MON_REFUND_DENIED` **242** reviews, **11.80%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.01★, 2022-04-08 → 2026-05-11. `MON_MONEYBACK` **42** reviews, **2.05%** of 2,051 cite the advertised money-back guarantee as not honoured, often with its conditions: "Money back policy requires that you use the product for 21 days consecutively!" (#442, `10444922266`, lt, 1★). `EU_CONSUMER` **10** reviews, **0.49%** of 2,051 invoke the EU 14-day right of withdrawal: "by revoking your right to withdraw within 14 days as dictated under EU law" (#284, `10164682229`, nl, 1★).

Refunds do happen: `MON_REFUND_GIVEN` **10** reviews, **0.49%** of 2,051, mean 2.80★. "they refunded  the 29.99 USD and kept the 9.99 USD as I sign up for it  , so fair enough" (#1280, `11351607971`, ae, 3★). 3 of those came through a bank or PayPal, not Habio.

**Evidence:** `8546542410`, `10311381495`, `10608104015`, `10862843638`, `11004157724`, `11136886688`, `11225362605`, `11484621917`.

### Escalation beyond the app (high-priority)

`U_ESCALATION` **196** reviews, **9.56%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.05★, 2022-04-18 → 2026-05-11. `BANK_DISPUTE` **118** reviews, **5.75%** of 2,051; `REG_COMPLAINT` **94** reviews, **4.58%** of 2,051. Named bodies include the BBB, FTC, ACCC, state attorneys general, the EU consumer regulator, Spain's OCU, Chile's Sernac, Germany's Verbraucherzentrale, police and Apple:

> "let the Better Business Bureau, the FTC, and econsumer dot gov know" — #1437, `11521787038`, us, 1★

> "Es ist eine Abofalle die bereits der Polizei und der Verbraucherzentrale gemeldet wurde." — #1149, `11216322956`, de, 1★
> *(translation: It is a subscription trap that has already been reported to the police and the consumer protection agency.)*

**Evidence:** `8581410208`, `10270571607`, `10607823887`, `10850861915`, `11002887130`, `11144563024`, `11277030472`, `11503780342`.

## 3.3 What goes wrong — the product

`U_PRODUCT_NEG` **257** reviews, **12.53%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.25★, 2021-04-06 → 2026-01-12. Non-seeded share 17.31%.

| Code | Meaning | n | % of 2,051 | Band | Mean ★ |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| `NEG_SHALLOW` | Basic / useless / not worth it | 102 | 4.97% | Very strong signal | 1.04 |
| `NEG_CONTENT_THIN` | Content generic / googleable / poorly written | 57 | 2.78% | Meaningful signal | 1.11 |
| `NEG_COMPLEX` | Overwhelming / confusing | 49 | 2.39% | Meaningful signal | 1.37 |
| `NEG_UX` | Clunky / poor UX | 38 | 1.85% | Meaningful signal | 1.61 |
| `NEG_ONBOARD_LONG` | Onboarding / quiz too long | 29 | 1.41% | Meaningful signal | 1.24 |
| `NEG_POPUP` | Too many pop-ups / prompts | 19 | 0.93% | Emerging signal | 1.32 |
| `NEG_NO_CUSTOM_HABIT` | Cannot create own habits | 9 | 0.44% | Weak signal | 1.22 |
| `NEG_FORCED_FLOW` | Forced steps (intentions, rewards, reflections) | 7 | 0.34% | Weak signal | 2.00 |

- **Shallow for the price.** "You literally pay to track your own habits." (#663, `10813939995`, us, 1★); "essentially charging 1 euro per page" (#1576, `11664580165`, it, 1★).
- **Generic or poor content.** "I should’ve picked up on something being off as soon as I saw all the typos and language errors." (#886, `11043750732`, us, 1★); a therapist: "which I would never recommend for a client or do myself" (#649, `10800744729`, us, 1★).
- **Overwhelming for the ADHD users it targets.** "For someone with ADHD, this app is way too overwhelming." (#731, `10894127344`, us, 2★).
- **Preset routines only.** "What if I want the routine to include a workout but not meditation?" (#1099, `11189301426`, us, 1★).

**Evidence:** `7189146685`, `9039125410`, `10228523192`, `10501202000`, `10756364742`, `10918430094`, `11038349650`, `11145291431`, `11237041111`, `11534025304`.

## 3.4 What goes wrong — reliability and access

`U_BUG_ANY` **196** reviews, **9.56%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.20★, 2020-11-10 → 2026-07-03. The defect that matters commercially is **paid but locked out**: `U_ACCESS_FAIL` **97** reviews, **4.73%** of 2,051 — Very strong signal, mean 1.02★, 2022-04-11 → 2025-03-18.

| Code | Meaning | n | % of 2,051 | Band | Mean ★ |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| `BUG_LOGIN` | Cannot log in / account or email not found | 52 | 2.54% | Meaningful signal | 1.04 |
| `BUG_ENTITLEMENT` | Paid but no premium access / restore fails | 43 | 2.10% | Meaningful signal | 1.00 |
| `BUG_ONBOARD_STUCK` | Stuck in onboarding / cannot proceed | 24 | 1.17% | Meaningful signal | 1.29 |
| `BUG_LAUNCH` | Won't open / white or black screen | 18 | 0.88% | Emerging signal | 1.06 |
| `BUG_GENERIC` | Doesn't work (unspecified) | 29 | 1.41% | Meaningful signal | 1.38 |
| `BUG_PERF` | Slow / laggy | 15 | 0.73% | Emerging signal | 1.20 |

> "when I tried to click the restore purchase button it says they couldn’t find any purchase history" — #264, `10102515955`, ph, 1★

> "die App zeigt dann immer an dass die Mail Adresse nicht gefunden wurde, wenn man kündigen will" — #1853, `12082860952`, de, 1★
> *(translation: the app always says the email address was not found when you try to cancel)*

Early-era defects were different: `BUG_ONBOARD_STUCK` 24 (first 2020-12-25, 15 in E1) and `BUG_LAYOUT` 8: "the “i need to change something” button is overlapping the start button" (#28, `7099613318`, au, 4★).

**Evidence:** `8556042897`, `10212438359`, `10379155958`, `10644396645`, `10800107353`, `11002558889`, `11116084699`, `11138835485`, `11196799337`, `11546467923`.

## 3.5 Unmet needs — the request list

`U_REQ_ANY` **82** reviews, **4.00%** of 2,051 — Very strong signal, mean 2.60★, 2021-01-13 → 2025-08-10. Requests are few because most reviewers never reached normal use.

| Code | Meaning | n | % of 2,051 | Band | Mean ★ |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---:|
| `REQ_LANG` | Wants other languages | 41 | 2.00% | Meaningful signal | 2.41 |
| `REQ_CUSTOM_ROUTINE` | Wants editable / own routines, more customisation | 18 | 0.88% | Emerging signal | 3.06 |
| `REQ_HELP` | Needs instructions / guidance | 8 | 0.39% | Weak signal | 2.00 |
| `REQ_CALENDAR` | Wants calendar view | 5 | 0.24% | Weak signal | 1.80 |
| `MON_TRIAL_REQ` | Wants a (free) trial before paying | 14 | 0.68% | Emerging signal | 1.14 |
| `MON_ONETIME_REQ` | Wants lifetime / one-time purchase | 2 | 0.10% | Ignore by default | 4.00 |
| `REQ_IPAD` | iPad / multi-device | 2 | 0.10% | Ignore by default | 3.00 |
| `REQ_RESET` | Reset progress / selections | 2 | 0.10% | Ignore by default | 1.00 |

Language is the only request at meaningful-signal strength. It predates the language-bait ads: 6 in E1, e.g. "ojalá pronto la pongan en español también" *(I hope they put it in Spanish soon)* (#26, `7084404117`, mx, 4★).

**Evidence:** `6863614321`, `7915137610`, `8684125309`, `9009285280`, `10113390555`, `11048635172`, `11141771684`, `11204107518`, `11427700669`, `11937120437`.

## 3.6 Dark patterns — reviewers' own word

`NEG_DARKUX` **163** reviews, **7.95%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.01★, 2023-07-06 → 2025-09-25. The term is used by reviewers who say they work in UX: "As a UX designer I should have seen the dark patterns from the start." (#751, `10917895569`, us, 1★); "환불을 어렵게 만드는 전형적인 다크 패턴" *(the classic dark pattern of making refunds hard)* (#502, `10576482476`, kr, 1★).

**Evidence:** `10108576912`, `10600303228`, `10767167713`, `10900043642`, `10970253662`, `11067949849`, `11156783951`, `11233029778`, `11359863927`, `11528442010`.

## 3.7 Churn

`U_CHURN` **75** reviews, **3.66%** of 2,051 — Very strong signal, mean 1.27★, 2021-04-19 → 2026-01-12. `CHURN_DELETE` 66, `CHURN_CANCEL` 6, `CHURN_RISK` 2, `CHURN_BLOCKED` 1. Reviews also stop would-be customers before they pay: `MKT_REVIEW_MISLEAD` **74** reviews, **3.61%** of 2,051 refer to other reviews, mostly as a warning. "The fact that the first several top-rated reviews were all previous users warning others not to pay for subscription" (#396, `10345521492`, us, 1★).

# PART 4 — RATINGS ANALYSIS

## 4.1 The distribution

| ★ | All | % of 2,051 | Flagged `SUS_SEED` | Non-seeded | % of non-seeded |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 5 | 610 | 29.74% | 540 | 70 | 4.79% |
| 4 | 77 | 3.75% | 48 | 29 | 1.98% |
| 3 | 25 | 1.22% | 0 | 25 | 1.71% |
| 2 | 44 | 2.15% | 1 | 43 | 2.94% |
| 1 | 1,295 | 63.14% | 0 | 1,295 | 88.58% |
| **mean** | **2.35** | | **4.91** | **1.31** | |

Without the flagged group, **88.58%** of reviews are one-star. The corpus has almost no middle: 2★–4★ together are 7.12%.

## 4.2 Themes by star band

### 5★ — 610 reviews (29.74%), 540 flagged

| Code | Meaning | n | % of 5★ reviews | Band |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| `PR_DESIGN` | Design / interface praised | 117 | 19.18% | High-priority signal |
| `PR_SIMPLE` | Easy to use / simple | 108 | 17.70% | High-priority signal |
| `PR_GENERIC` | Generic praise | 99 | 16.23% | High-priority signal |
| `PR_MOTIVATION` | Motivating | 79 | 12.95% | High-priority signal |
| `PR_OUTCOME` | Reports a real outcome / habit built | 68 | 11.15% | High-priority signal |
| `PR_STATS` | Progress tracking / analytics | 64 | 10.49% | High-priority signal |
| `PR_CONTENT` | Articles / courses / quotes / insights | 48 | 7.87% | High-priority signal |
| `PR_CUSTOM` | Customisable / unlimited habits | 47 | 7.70% | High-priority signal |
| `PR_NOTIF` | Reminders praised | 43 | 7.05% | High-priority signal |
| `PR_JOURNAL` | Journal / daily reflection praised | 40 | 6.56% | High-priority signal |

**Why 5★.** In the flagged group: design, ease, motivation, stats, in brochure language. In the **70 non-seeded 5★ reviews**, the largest share is from the free era (26 of 70 in E1) and praise the app being free, its look, or a personal outcome: "Let’s you have as many habits you want for free." (#21, `6953400305`, us, 5★); "a super useful addition to my adhd treatment plan" (#185, `9078274916`, ca, 5★). 5 of them are complaints under a 5★ (§4.3).

Median one-star body length is 205 characters, against 96 for non-seeded 5★: these are detailed accounts, not drive-by ratings.

## 4.3 Rating-versus-text contradictions

`CONTRA_RATING` **8** reviews, **0.39%** of 2,051. Mostly 5★ or 4★ bodies that are purely complaints ("review said free but it’s not free" (#83, `8311453620`, gb, 5★)), plus one 2★ that is pure praise. `CONTRA_TEXT` 2: a 5★ titled "stopped working" (#1960, `12178729000`, us, 5★) and "IT IS FREE" (#1951, `12167792091`, gb, 5★). `CONTRA_DISSENT` 1: the one reviewer who disputes the scam consensus (#1244, `11304376291`, fr, 4★). All are kept and counted by their text.

**All contradiction IDs:** `8311453620`, `10153379088`, `10687357788`, `10864877636`, `11011058403`, `11083723256`, `11304376291`, `11487584408`, `12150557354`, `12167792091`, `12178729000`.

# PART 5 — PAID-USER AND UPGRADE ANALYSIS

## 5.1 The population, and what it is not

Two populations are kept apart:

- **Money left their account: 995 reviewers (48.51% of 2,051; 68.06% of non-seeded)**, mean 1.05★. This is the union of any first-person charge, paid trial, deliberate purchase, refund refusal or grant, web-funnel payment, and paid-but-locked-out. Most did not intend the charge they complain about.
- **Deliberate purchasers: `MON_PAID_EXPLICIT` 35 (1.71%)**, mean 1.23★: reviewers who say they chose to buy a subscription or product, beyond a trial.

**Neither is a conversion rate.** Reviewers are a self-selected minority of customers, and a charge dispute is a strong reason to write. No revenue, conversion or refund-rate figure is derivable or claimed.

## 5.2 What triggers a purchase

The corpus describes one dominant path, in this order:

1. **An ad** on Instagram or Facebook: `MKT_INSTAGRAM` 40, `MKT_FACEBOOK` 4, `MKT_SOCIAL` 8. The pitch is usually ADHD help, a reading hack or a personality profile (§0.4).
2. **A quiz**, often in the viewer's language, that promises a personalised plan or report: `MKT_AD_PERSONALITY` 31, `NEG_ONBOARD_LONG` 29. "so we don’t waste our time filling up a bunch of useless data in the first place" (#101, `8413545877`, ar, 1★).
3. **A small paid trial**: `MON_TRIAL_1DOLLAR` 148. The low price is itself the reason given for trying: "€5.49 seemed like a reasonable amount to see if the app could fulfil on the promises made in the advertising and offer." (#926, `11079433591`, gb, 1★).
4. **An immediate add-on offer** (workbook/report): `MON_WORKBOOK` 189.
5. **Auto-renewal** into a monthly, 6-monthly or annual plan: `MON_TRIAL_CHARGE` 173.

Very few reviews describe choosing to buy because of the product itself: "I liked it so much I purchased a yearly subscription" (#2032, `12435693615`, us, 3★); "Es ist das Geld Wert!" *(It is worth the money!)* (#1343, `11421747934`, de, 5★).

## 5.3 Segment rates among reviewers whose money left their account

Denominator: **995** money-left reviewers. Global % shown for comparison. These are segment rates, not corpus signal bands.

| Theme | n in segment | % of segment | % of all 2,051 | Lift |

| `MON_CANCEL` | 284 | 28.5% | 17.6% | 1.62× |
| `MON_REFUND_DENIED` | 242 | 24.3% | 11.8% | 2.06× |
| `U_SUPPORT_FAIL` | 375 | 37.7% | 20.7% | 1.82× |

| `BANK_DISPUTE` | 104 | 10.5% | 5.8% | 1.82× |
| `REG_COMPLAINT` | 76 | 7.6% | 4.6% | 1.67× |
| `U_ACCESS_FAIL` | 81 | 8.1% | 4.7% | 1.72× |

## 5.4 What deliberate purchasers report

Among the 35 `MON_PAID_EXPLICIT` reviewers (segment rates of 35):

| Code | Meaning | n | % of paid reviewers | Band |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| `MON_REFUND_DENIED` | Refund refused | 6 | 17.14% | High-priority signal |
| `NEG_CONTENT_THIN` | Content generic / googleable / poorly written | 5 | 14.29% | High-priority signal |
| `ACC_SCAM` | Calls the app a scam / fraud / theft | 5 | 14.29% | High-priority signal |
| `MKT_AD_MISLEAD` | Ad did not match the product | 5 | 14.29% | High-priority signal |
| `NEG_SHALLOW` | Basic / useless / not worth it | 5 | 14.29% | High-priority signal |
| `MON_REFUND_SEEK` | Requests a refund (outcome not stated) | 5 | 14.29% | High-priority signal |
| `MKT_LANG_BAIT` | Ad/quiz in local language, app English-only | 4 | 11.43% | High-priority signal |
| `BUG_LOGIN` | Cannot log in / account or email not found | 4 | 11.43% | High-priority signal |
| `SUP_NONE` | No response from support | 4 | 11.43% | High-priority signal |
| `BUG_ENTITLEMENT` | Paid but no premium access / restore fails | 4 | 11.43% | High-priority signal |
| `SUP_SLOW` | Slow support response | 4 | 11.43% | High-priority signal |
| `MKT_INSTAGRAM` | Came from Instagram | 4 | 11.43% | High-priority signal |

The buyers who paid on purpose report the same failures as everyone else, plus locked premium: "I paid for a premium access and I was not able to access the premium features." (#385, `10322014405`, ae, 1★); "Doesn't even have an option to change the theme or profile photo." (#141, `8745903782`, ch, 1★).

## 5.5 Upgrade barriers among non-buyers

`U_PAYWALL` **129** reviews, **6.29%** of 2,051 — High-priority signal, mean 1.28★, 2021-04-24 → 2026-07-30. In E2, when the free tier disappeared, `MON_HARDWALL` was 17.74% of that era. The objection was not price as such but paying before seeing anything: "I’d love an opportunity to try before I buy!" (#96, `8397732241`, gb, 1★); "consideren una que sea de un único pago o “Lifetime”, anima más a los usuarios." *(consider a one-off or lifetime payment; it encourages users more.)* (#18, `6863614321`, mx, 5★).

## 5.6 Refund and churn drivers after purchase

In descending order within the money-left segment: support failure (`U_SUPPORT_FAIL` 37.7%); cancellation trouble (`MON_CANCEL` 28.5%); refund refusal (`MON_REFUND_DENIED` 24.3%); the workbook charge (`MON_WORKBOOK` 19.0%); a product judged not worth the charge (`NEG_SHALLOW` 6.9%). The language mismatch is a refund trigger in its own right. Of `MKT_LANG_BAIT` reviewers, 34.9% ask for or are refused a refund: "año que les regale ya que nunca me contestaron para decirme si podía cambiar a español o no" *(a year I gave them, since they never answered whether I could switch to Spanish)* (#1164, `11224233255`, mx, 1★).

## 5.7 What cannot be claimed here

- How many customers are charged unexpectedly, refunded, or charge back.
- Whether the workbook screen or trial terms were ever changed. Only the timing of complaints is observable (§7).
- Whether retained subscribers are satisfied. The corpus contains almost none who say so outside the flagged group.

Seven storefronts clear 50 reviews: us (886), gb (159), fr (91), de (85), au (74), ca (69), es (53). Together they hold 1,417 reviews (69.09%). The other 74 contribute to every global number but get no standalone claims (§6.12).

The US public rating is 4.28★ from 3,932 ratings, the highest of the eight storefronts looked up. US reviewers name US bodies when they escalate (BBB, FTC, state attorney general): "I plan to also file with the attorney general." (#802, `10969072744`, us, 1★).

Reviewers write Abofalle (subscription trap) and Widerrufsfrist (withdrawal period): "obwohl ich innerhalb der Widerrufsfrust sogar mit nachweislichen Screenshots gekündigt habe" *(even though I cancelled within the withdrawal period, with screenshots as proof)* (#2047, `14053313423`, de, 1★).

## 6.10 High-spend and high-review-volume markets

**No consumer-spend dataset was fetched in this session**, so no spend ranking is asserted. Two groups are defined from sources that were captured:

- **High-spend proxy group** = the eligible storefronts with the most public ratings in the Apple lookup API (an install-base proxy, not spend): us (3,932), gb (896), ca (550), au (407), fr (302), de (291).
- **High-review-volume group** = the seven storefronts with ≥50 reviews in this corpus: us, gb, fr, de, au, ca, es.

| Group | Reviews | Mean ★ | Non-seeded mean ★ | Billing harm | Cancel trap | Support fail | Ad mismatch | Praise (non-seeded) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| High-spend proxy (6) | 1,364 | 2.78 | 1.30 | 34.8% | 25.7% | 19.8% | 8.2% | 7.7% |
| High-review-volume (7) | 1,417 | 2.72 | 1.29 | 36.6% | 26.1% | 19.8% | 8.7% | 7.2% |
| All other storefronts (74) | 634 | 1.53 | 1.35 | 56.8% | 35.8% | 22.7% | 11.4% | 10.1% |
| Global | 2,051 | 2.35 | 1.31 | 42.8% | 29.1% | 20.7% | 9.5% | 8.4% |

**Reading:** the complaint does not depend on the market. Non-seeded means sit near 1★ in every group, and in every eligible storefront the largest complaint theme is billing harm or the cancellation trap. What varies is the *mechanism*: workbook (gb, es), language bait (fr, de), cancellation and support (au, de). The flagged 5★ wave is almost entirely a US phenomenon, with a small echo on the Russian storefront (§6.11).

# PART 7 — TIME-TREND ANALYSIS

## 7.1 Method

Eras are cut at points where the dominant complaint changes, identified from the monthly series in §7.3 and fixed *before* the era tables were computed. The timeline checks in §9.C assert the boundaries that matter (e.g. no `MKT_LANG_BAIT` before 2024-03, no `SUS_SEED` outside 2024-03 → 2025-02).

| Era | Name | Dates | Reviews | Mean ★ | 1★ % | 5★ % | Flagged | Non-seeded mean ★ |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| E1 | Free/early | 2020-10-18 → 2021-12-30 | 73 | 3.03 | 32.9% | 35.6% | 0 | 3.03 |
| E2 | Paywall arrives | 2022-01-04 → 2023-06-30 | 186 | 1.73 | 73.7% | 11.8% | 0 | 1.73 |
| E3 | Web funnel + off-store billing | 2023-07-01 → 2024-02-29 | 572 | 1.17 | 93.4% | 2.3% | 0 | 1.17 |
| E4 | Workbook, language-bait and 5★ wave | 2024-03-01 → 2025-02-19 | 1,198 | 2.98 | 48.7% | 45.7% | 589 | 1.11 |
| E5 | Tail | 2025-03-08 → 2026-07-30 | 22 | 1.59 | 77.3% | 9.1% | 0 | 1.59 |

## 7.4 What changed

**E1 (free, 73 reviews, 3.03★).** A free app with a thin free tier and layout bugs that trapped people in onboarding (`BUG_ONBOARD_STUCK` 15 of 73 E1 (20.55%)). Praise was real and specific: `U_PRAISE_ANY` 39 of 73 E1 (53.42%).

**E2 (paywall, 186 reviews, 1.73★).** The free tier disappears: `MON_HARDWALL` 33 of 186 E2 (17.74%). Charges after cancelling begin (`MON_CHARGED_AFTER` 43 of 186 E2 (23.12%)), first on 2022-02-24. Reviewers who read an older "it's free" review arrive angry: "I downloaded this app based on the pinned review that stated it had a free version" (#105, `8420684349`, gb, 2★).

**E3 (web funnel, 572 reviews, 1.17★).** Volume triples. Off-store billing (`MON_OFFSTORE` 79 of 572 E3 (13.81%)), the $1 trial (`MON_TRIAL_1DOLLAR` 74 of 572 E3 (12.94%)) and the bionic-reading ad (`MKT_AD_BIONIC` 18 of 572 E3 (3.15%)) define the era; scam language reaches 51.4% of reviews. The workbook appears from 2023-10-15.

**E4 (workbook, language, 5★ wave, 1,198 reviews, 2.98★; non-seeded 1.11★).** Workbook complaints peak (2024-04: 41); language-bait ads begin (2024-03-28) and account for `MKT_LANG_BAIT` 85 of 1,198 E4 (7.10%). From 2024-03-11 the flagged 5★ reviews arrive, peaking in 2024-12 (101). From 2024-08 they outnumber one-star reviews in every month with ≥5 reviews, and the era's mean rises to 2.98★ while the non-seeded mean stays at 1.11★.

**E5 (tail, 22 reviews, 1.59★; limited evidence).** The flagged reviews stop after 2025-02-18. What remains is sparse: the same billing complaints, a genuine dashboard critique (#2045, `13626218768`, za, 1★) and one warm 5★ (#2046, `13944588671`, us, 5★).

## 7.5 What persisted

- **Cancellation and charges after cancelling**, from 2022-02-24 to 2025-09-25, in every era from E2.
- **Language requests**, from E1 (6) to E5 (2). The ads made it worse; they did not create it.
- **Liking the look while leaving**: `PR_DESIGN` appears in non-seeded reviews in every era (E1 16, E2 12, E3 3, E4 5, E5 3).

## 7.6 Sensitivity — does one episode drive the conclusions?

| Headline share | All 2,051 | Non-seeded | Excluding E3 | Excluding E4 | Excluding E3 and E4 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `U_BILLING_HARM` | 42.8% | 60.1% | 32.7% | 54.0% | 23.5% |
| `U_CANCEL_TRAP` | 29.1% | 40.8% | 21.0% | 41.3% | 23.1% |
| `U_SUPPORT_FAIL` | 20.7% | 29.1% | 15.1% | 28.7% | 15.7% |
| `ACC_SCAM` | 33.3% | 46.6% | 26.2% | 39.4% | 14.9% |
| `U_AD_MISMATCH` | 9.5% | 13.2% | 9.3% | 8.2% | 4.6% |
| `U_PRAISE_ANY` | 32.6% | 8.4% | 43.4% | 12.4% | 28.5% |

Billing harm stays high-priority under every exclusion. The positive share is the number that moves: it depends almost entirely on the flagged E4 reviews.

# PART 8 — PRODUCT IMPLICATIONS

Each item cites the findings it rests on. **Immediate fixes** address harm that is already a legal and platform risk; **experiments** test a hypothesis; **research questions** need data the corpus cannot give.

## 8.1 Immediate fix — one visible, one-tap cancellation for every subscription, wherever it was bought (§0.2, §3.2)

- Show web-funnel subscriptions in the app's settings with status, next charge date and amount; make "Cancel" one tap with an on-screen and emailed confirmation that states no further charge will occur. Rests on `U_CANCEL_TRAP` 597, `MON_OFFSTORE` 137, `MON_CHARGED_AFTER` 351.
- Treat an emailed cancellation request as effective **at receipt**, not at the time support replies (§0.6).
- Remove expiring cancellation links (#543, `10647307854`, au, 1★) and "email not found" dead ends (#1853, `12082860952`, de, 1★).
- Align the listing text with how web subscriptions are actually billed and cancelled (§2.2).

## 8.2 Immediate fix — the workbook upsell needs its own confirmation, price and refund path (§0.3)

- Put the price on the button, require a separate confirmation (not the trial's stored authorisation), default to "No thanks", and make repeat taps idempotent. Rests on `MON_WORKBOOK` 189, `MON_DOUBLECHARGE` 157, `NEG_DARKUX` 163.
- Refund an unopened PDF on request. Reviewers paste the "we do not offer refunds for in-app purchases" reply verbatim (#1095, `11188294921`, mx, 1★; #1191, `11245047696`, es, 1★), and in EU storefronts it collides with withdrawal rights (`EU_CONSUMER` 10).

## 8.3 Immediate fix — trials that cannot surprise (§3.2)

- A reminder 48 hours before conversion, showing the renewal amount in local currency and plan length. Rests on `MON_TRIAL_CHARGE` 173, `MON_NO_RECEIPT` 68, `MON_PRICE_MISMATCH` 55.
- Make the trial longer than support's own response time (`MON_TRIAL_SHORT` 9; 19 reviews quote the 4-business-day SLA against a 3-day trial).

## 8.4 Immediate fix — a paid customer must be able to get in (§3.4)

- Link web purchases to app accounts automatically, and make "Restore purchases" find them. Rests on `U_ACCESS_FAIL` 97 (`BUG_LOGIN` 52, `BUG_ENTITLEMENT` 43, `BUG_CONTENT` 10).

## 8.5 Immediate fix — stop promising what the app does not contain (§0.4)

- Either localise the app or state "English only" in every non-English ad, quiz and checkout. Rests on `MKT_LANG_BAIT` 86 and `REQ_LANG` 41; France 33.0%.
- Withdraw bionic-reading and personality-report creative unless those features ship (`MKT_AD_BIONIC` 22, `MKT_AD_PERSONALITY` 31).
- Re-examine "designed in collaboration with mental health professionals" against the ADHD segment's experience (§0.5).

## 8.6 Immediate fix — human support inside the trial window (§0.6)

- First human reply within 24 hours; no retention questions in reply to a cancellation request; no identical public replies under reviews. Rests on `U_SUPPORT_FAIL` 425, `SUP_TEMPLATE` 59, `SUP_EVASIVE` 46.

## 8.7 Immediate fix — review integrity (§0.7, §9.H)

- Any programme that sources, incentivises or scripts App Store reviews should stop. The corpus shows the pattern in 589 reviews, including duplicated text, a pasted developer reply and referral spam. Customers already cite the gap between rating and reviews as evidence of fraud (`MKT_FAKE_REVIEWS` 12), and it adds platform-policy risk to the `REG_COMPLAINT` 94 escalations already filed. This recommendation concerns the pattern; the corpus cannot say who wrote the reviews.

## 8.8 Experiment — a real free tier or free trial, with no card upfront (§5.5, §7.4)

- E1 praise was specifically about free access (`PR_FREE` 9); E2 objections were about paying blind (`MON_HARDWALL` 47, `MON_TRIAL_REQ` 14). Hypothesis: a card-free trial lowers disputes more than it lowers paid conversion. Measure chargebacks and refund requests, not only conversion.

## 8.9 Experiment — onboarding built for the ADHD audience the ads target (§0.5, §3.3)

- A short path to the first tracked habit, skippable pop-ups, and the quiz after first value rather than before payment. Rests on `NEG_COMPLEX` 49, `NEG_ONBOARD_LONG` 29, `NEG_POPUP` 19; ADHD segment mean 1.25★.

## 8.10 Experiment — editable routines and own habits (§3.5)

- `REQ_CUSTOM_ROUTINE` 18 + `NEG_NO_CUSTOM_HABIT` 9. Modest signal, but it is the most consistent product request from people who got past the paywall.

## 8.11 What the corpus says to keep

- The calm, soft visual design: non-seeded `PR_DESIGN` 39 across every era, often from complainants (§0.8). The upsell-heavy dashboard (#2045, `13626218768`, za, 1★) works against it.
- The daily reflection journal (#2046, `13944588671`, us, 5★).

## 8.12 Research questions the corpus cannot answer

1. What share of customers is charged after cancelling, and what is the chargeback rate by acquisition channel (web vs App Store)?
2. What share of workbook purchases are refunded, disputed or never opened?
3. What is the rating and retention of customers who bought in-app versus via the web funnel?
4. Among genuinely retained subscribers, what do they value? The corpus holds too few non-flagged positive reviews to say.
5. What were the ad creatives and languages by market and month, to confirm the timing in §7.3?

# PART 9 — EVIDENCE APPENDIX

## 9.A Counting rules

1. **Denominator.** Global = 2,051. Non-seeded = 1,462. Country, star band, era and segment denominators are stated at each use.
2. **Non-exclusive.** A review is counted once in each theme it carries. Percentages sum past 100%.
3. **One code per review per theme.**
4. **Codes come from the text, never from the star rating.** A 5★ whose text is a complaint is coded as a complaint and flagged `CONTRA_RATING`.
5. **First-person only for charges.** A reviewer relaying others' charges is not coded `MON_CHARGED_*` (§9.C, #1329).
6. **Requests vs defects.** "It doesn't have X" is `REQ_*`; "X doesn't work" is `BUG_*`; "X isn't worth it" is `NEG_SHALLOW`; a price objection is `MON_*`.
7. **`MON_PAID_EXPLICIT` requires a statement of deliberate purchase** beyond a trial.
8. **`SUS_SEED` is hand judgement** applied to short, generic, brochure-like 4–5★ reviews in the 2024-03 → 2025-02 cluster. It was never applied to a review describing a concrete personal incident, and it never removes a review from any count.
9. **Unions are derived, never hand-applied**; mechanical codes (`STAR_n`, `ERA_En`) come from the record.
10. **Signal bands** are computed from count ÷ denominator using §1.5.

## 9.C Validation — 765 checks, 0 failures

`67-validate.py` output, verbatim:

```
765 checks, 0 failures
  coverage: 8
  codes: 4
  theme_map: 507
  unions: 28
  mech: 6
  timeline: 7
  semantic: 6
  lang: 6
  bounds: 193
```

The timeline checks were written before Part 7 and would fail if its era narrative were back-fitted: `MON_WORKBOOK` none before 2023-10; `MKT_LANG_BAIT` none before 2024-03; `MKT_AD_BIONIC` none before 2023-07; `BUG_LAYOUT` none after 2023; `SUS_SEED` only 2024-03 → 2025-02; `PR_FREE` majority pre-2022.

### What earlier runs caught

1. **#1517 language tag** — tagged Japanese because of the jp storefront; the review is English. Tag and note corrected. Found by the script-presence check.
2. **`PR_FREE` timeline check was wrong, not the data.** 6 of 9 `PR_FREE` reviews predate 2022. The exceptions are #275, `10147617028`, us, 5★ and #406, `10374364053`, us, 5★ (2023, consistent with users who closed the paywall, cf. #188, `9089547600`, gb, 5★) and #1951, `12167792091`, gb, 5★ (`CONTRA_TEXT`). The threshold was changed to a majority.
3. **#1329** carried `MON_CHARGED_NOSUB` for hearsay ("I've seen a few reviews saying…"). Removed under rule 9.A.5.
4. **#1240** carried a non-standard language tag; corrected to `en`.
5. While writing batch 14, #1329 was typed with #1330's review ID. Caught by re-reading the index before the alignment check ran, and corrected.

### What validation cannot check

Whether a code is the *right* judgement. That is auditable only by reading the note on each line of `67-cls/`, which quotes the evidence.

## 9.D Sensitivity

See §7.6. Three further stress tests of the headline `U_BILLING_HARM` share, each removing a group whose account could be argued away:

| Removed | Reviews removed | `U_BILLING_HARM` share of remaining | Band |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Reviews that cite other reviews (possible echo) | 74 | 830 of 1,977 (41.98%) | High-priority signal |
| Billing reviews whose only mechanism is trial conversion (could be misread terms) | 130 | 748 of 1,921 (38.94%) | High-priority signal |
| Billing reviews whose only mechanism is the workbook (could be a mis-tap) | 86 | 792 of 1,965 (40.31%) | High-priority signal |
| All three together plus all flagged reviews | 861 | 632 of 1,190 (53.11%) | High-priority signal |

The finding survives every removal at high-priority strength.

## 9.H Burst, solicitation and authenticity checks

| Check | Result | Reading |
|---|---|---|
| Repeat `(country, author)` pairs | 0 | none within a storefront |
| Author names on two storefronts | 5 | vikusavikas: #1714 us 5★, #1719 ru 5★; Mil.ash: #1737 us 5★, #1738 ru 5★; inv88: #1925 us 5★, #2028 ru 5★; amatoriya2022: #1580 gb 5★, #1601 us 5★; DylCpt: #614 za 1★, #695 us 1★ | 4 of 5 are both-flagged pairs |
| Duplicate bodies (≥25 chars) across accounts | 12 reviews in 6 pairs | #1841 #1842 #1843 #1844 #1849 #1852 #1864 #1865 #1866 #1867 #1869 #1871; all 5★/4★, all 2024-12, four days apart |
| Split paragraph | 3 | #1799 #1800 #1801 |
| Developer reply pasted into review | 1 | #1846 |
| Busiest day | 2024-04-08 — 13 reviews | 5★ 1, 4★ 1, 3★ 1, 1★ 10 — a negative burst |
| Busiest month | 2024-04 — 208 reviews | 1★ 179, 5★ 17 |
| Flagged reviews per month | peak 2024-12 (101) | first 2024-03-11, last 2025-02-18 |
| Flagged 5★ with a charge/refund/cancel/support code | 0 | flagged reviews never describe an incident |
| Non-flagged 4–5★ from 2024-03 onward | 16 | #841 #854 #983 #1128 #1244 #1343 #1525 #1734 #1806 #1832 #1839 #1938 #1951 #1976 #2030 #2046 (language requests 4: #1128 #1734 #1839 #1938; refund or resolution thanks 2: #983 #1525; no content 3: #1806 #1832 #2030; rating contradicts text 2: #854 #1951; dissent from the scam consensus 1: #1244; short generic praise 2: #841 #1976; substantive praise 2: #1343 #2046) |
| Reviews with votes | 259 (12.63%) | max 16 |
| Edited reviews | 38 | several narrate unresolved follow-ups |
| `CONTRA_RATING` | 8 | §4.3 |

**Borderline cases, stated:** #841, `11002604143`, us, 5★ (2024-03-03, short generic 5★ just before the cluster) is *not* flagged; #1098, `11188980676`, us, 5★ and #1116, `11197896433`, us, 5★ (mild criticism in the flagged style) *are* flagged. Moving all three changes the flagged count by at most 3.

## 9.I Developer responsiveness — the evidence both ways

Developer replies are not in the dataset. Reviewers describe them: 19 reviews refer to a public reply under reviews, typically an instruction to email support, and say the email then went unanswered (`10093276946`, `10297726621`, `10389726314`, `10727767276`, `10874689453`, `11188294921`, `11697740607`, `11703703549`). Support resolution is reported in `SUP_RESOLVED` **6** reviews, **0.29%** of 2,051 and refunds in `MON_REFUND_GIVEN` **10** reviews, **0.49%** of 2,051. Positive support statements (`SUP_GOOD` 18) all come from flagged reviews (18).
